229Q proves an exact CM rationalization on E_d:Y^2=X^3-d^2X: for P in E_d(Q(i)), A=P+conj(P) and C=iota(P-conj(P)), iota(X,Y)=(-X,iY), lie in E_d(Q), with 2P=A-iota(C).
